# Master-pack asset catalog
Auto-generated by `scripts/build_asset_catalog.py`. **Do not edit by hand** —
extend the generator (or the inputs it reads) instead.

## Sources
* `unpacked/overlay/_manifest.json` — 347 master-pack resources (the universe).
* `ghidra_analysis/asset_catalog/_cache/ghidra_xrefs.jsonl` — every instruction
  immediate **and** every `.data`/`.rdata` u32 that holds a resource ID,
  dumped from the live Ghidra DB.
* `bulanci.ghidra.exe.c` / `bulanci.ida.exe.c` — text decompilations, used to
  pin a literal to a specific source line for browsing.
* `ghidra_analysis/**/*.md` — every existing analysis note that mentions a
  resource by hex ID or by unpacked filename.
* `unpacked/<pack>/*.script.asm` — every script disassembly (master-pack
  scripts under `overlay/`, plus 15 per-level scripts under `<level>_eap/`),
  mining `LoadPreface(id)`, `SetMusic(id, ...)`, `CreateImage(... id)`,
  `CreateAnim(... frames=[id, ...])` calls for master-pack ID literals.

## Coverage
* Total resources: **347**
* With *any* xref (incl. master-pack overlay-loader synthetics): **347** (100.0%)
* With *concrete* evidence (instruction, data, decomp text, analysis-md, script, or traced runtime mechanism like class
  enumeration / audio-bank mirror): **344** (99.1%)
* **Orphans (no traced loader)**: **3** resources are present
  in the master pack but no static evidence ties them to a specific
  loader function. Split into three sub-categories:
  * **Dead / unreferenced**: **3** resources confirmed by
    exhaustive verification to have ZERO references anywhere
    (Ghidra instruction sweep, .data/.rdata sweep, ghidra+ida
    decompilations, every `.script.asm` under `unpacked/`,
    every analysis `.md`, and the embedded `BULANCI.TMP`
    installer PE). Engine still allocates a pool slot for them
    at boot — they're sponsor leftovers / removed-feature
    assets that nothing in the code path ever consumes.
  * **Adjacency-only**: **0** resources sit in the
    middle of a same-class run whose neighbours ARE concretely traced;
    each one carries a `sequence-neighbour` synthetic xref naming
    that neighbour, but exhaustive scanning has confirmed the
    neighbour's loader function does NOT contain the orphan's ID
    anywhere — the actual loader is still to be identified.
  * **Overlay-only**: **0** resources have no literal
    reference anywhere in the binary (byte-level confirmed). They're
    attributed to `CDSApp_AppMain` as the engine entry that maps the
    master-pack overlay into the resource pool.

  Each orphan's detail section carries an `_orphan_` marker.
  **Do not** infer the loader function from the adjacency hint when
  pushing names back into Ghidra — verify with disassembly first.

* With Ghidra data xref: 146
